 Dataset: ../../datasets/issue83_pack_split.dat
============================================================

 How to read this output
   GOPs   [billions of operations per second = 2*M*N*K / time; a multiply-add counts as 2; higher is better]
   Timed  [pack timer + compute timer per rep -- inner timers; gaps between them are excluded; they cost 11-17%: DEBUG build, do not compare to the reference]
   Spread [each rep's own GOPs: the slowest, 5th percentile, 95th percentile and fastest rep, the width of the middle 50%,
           the standard deviation, and the fastest-to-median ratio; a wide spread means unstable timing]
   Full definitions of every log column: docs/metrics.md

============================================================
 Benchmark Config: Arch=jbpi2 | Datatype=fp32 | Method=AXPY_FP32_Pack | VLEN=256
 Dataset: ../../datasets/issue83_pack_split.dat
============================================================

 How to read this output
   GOPs   [billions of operations per second = 2*M*N*K / time; a multiply-add counts as 2; higher is better]
   Timed  [pack timer + compute timer per rep -- inner timers; gaps between them are excluded; they cost 11-17%: DEBUG build, do not compare to the reference]
   Spread [each rep's own GOPs: the slowest, 5th percentile, 95th percentile and fastest rep, the width of the middle 50%,
           the standard deviation, and the fastest-to-median ratio; a wide spread means unstable timing]
   Full definitions of every log column: docs/metrics.md

 Benchmark Config: Arch=jbpi2 | Datatype=fp32 | Method=AXPY_FP32_Pack | VLEN=256
 Dataset: ../../datasets/issue83_pack_split.dat
============================================================

 How to read this output
   GOPs   [billions of operations per second = 2*M*N*K / time; a multiply-add counts as 2; higher is better]
   Timed  [pack timer + compute timer per rep -- inner timers; gaps between them are excluded; they cost 11-17%: DEBUG build, do not compare to the reference]
   Spread [each rep's own GOPs: the slowest, 5th percentile, 95th percentile and fastest rep, the width of the middle 50%,
           the standard deviation, and the fastest-to-median ratio; a wide spread means unstable timing]
   Full definitions of every log column: docs/metrics.md

============================================================
 Benchmark Config: Arch=jbpi2 | Datatype=fp32 | Method=AXPY_FP32_Pack | VLEN=256
 Dataset: ../../datasets/issue83_pack_split.dat
============================================================

 How to read this output
   GOPs   [billions of operations per second = 2*M*N*K / time; a multiply-add counts as 2; higher is better]
   Timed  [pack timer + compute timer per rep -- inner timers; gaps between them are excluded; they cost 11-17%: DEBUG build, do not compare to the reference]
   Spread [each rep's own GOPs: the slowest, 5th percentile, 95th percentile and fastest rep, the width of the middle 50%,
           the standard deviation, and the fastest-to-median ratio; a wide spread means unstable timing]
   Full definitions of every log column: docs/metrics.md

 Dataset: ../../datasets/issue83_pack_split.dat

 How to read this output
   GOPs   [billions of operations per second = 2*M*N*K / time; a multiply-add counts as 2; higher is better]
   Timed  [pack timer + compute timer per rep -- inner timers; gaps between them are excluded; they cost 11-17%: DEBUG build, do not compare to the reference]
   Spread [each rep's own GOPs: the slowest, 5th percentile, 95th percentile and fastest rep, the width of the middle 50%,
           the standard deviation, and the fastest-to-median ratio; a wide spread means unstable timing]
   Full definitions of every log column: docs/metrics.md

============================================================
 Benchmark Config: Arch=jbpi2 | Datatype=fp32 | Method=AXPY_FP32_Pack | VLEN=256
 Dataset: ../../datasets/issue83_pack_split.dat
============================================================

 How to read this output
   GOPs   [billions of operations per second = 2*M*N*K / time; a multiply-add counts as 2; higher is better]
   Timed  [pack timer + compute timer per rep -- inner timers; gaps between them are excluded; they cost 11-17%: DEBUG build, do not compare to the reference]
   Spread [each rep's own GOPs: the slowest, 5th percentile, 95th percentile and fastest rep, the width of the middle 50%,
           the standard deviation, and the fastest-to-median ratio; a wide spread means unstable timing]
   Full definitions of every log column: docs/metrics.md

 Dataset: ../../datasets/issue83_pack_split.dat
============================================================

 How to read this output
   GOPs   [billions of operations per second = 2*M*N*K / time; a multiply-add counts as 2; higher is better]
   Timed  [pack timer + compute timer per rep -- inner timers; gaps between them are excluded; they cost 11-17%: DEBUG build, do not compare to the reference]
   Spread [each rep's own GOPs: the slowest, 5th percentile, 95th percentile and fastest rep, the width of the middle 50%,
           the standard deviation, and the fastest-to-median ratio; a wide spread means unstable timing]
   Full definitions of every log column: docs/metrics.md

============================================================

 Dataset: ../../datasets/issue83_pack_split.dat
============================================================

 How to read this output
   GOPs   [billions of operations per second = 2*M*N*K / time; a multiply-add counts as 2; higher is better]
   Timed  [pack timer + compute timer per rep -- inner timers; gaps between them are excluded; they cost 11-17%: DEBUG build, do not compare to the reference]
   Spread [each rep's own GOPs: the slowest, 5th percentile, 95th percentile and fastest rep, the width of the middle 50%,
           the standard deviation, and the fastest-to-median ratio; a wide spread means unstable timing]
   Full definitions of every log column: docs/metrics.md

============================================================

 Dataset: ../../datasets/issue83_pack_split.dat

 How to read this output
   GOPs   [billions of operations per second = 2*M*N*K / time; a multiply-add counts as 2; higher is better]
   Timed  [pack timer + compute timer per rep -- inner timers; gaps between them are excluded; they cost 11-17%: DEBUG build, do not compare to the reference]
   Spread [each rep's own GOPs: the slowest, 5th percentile, 95th percentile and fastest rep, the width of the middle 50%,
           the standard deviation, and the fastest-to-median ratio; a wide spread means unstable timing]
   Full definitions of every log column: docs/metrics.md

============================================================
 Benchmark Config: Arch=jbpi2 | Datatype=fp32 | Method=AXPY_FP32_Pack | VLEN=256
 Dataset: ../../datasets/issue83_pack_split.dat
============================================================

 How to read this output
   GOPs   [billions of operations per second = 2*M*N*K / time; a multiply-add counts as 2; higher is better]
   Timed  [pack timer + compute timer per rep -- inner timers; gaps between them are excluded; they cost 11-17%: DEBUG build, do not compare to the reference]
   Spread [each rep's own GOPs: the slowest, 5th percentile, 95th percentile and fastest rep, the width of the middle 50%,
           the standard deviation, and the fastest-to-median ratio; a wide spread means unstable timing]
   Full definitions of every log column: docs/metrics.md

============================================================
 Benchmark Config: Arch=jbpi2 | Datatype=fp32 | Method=AXPY_FP32_Pack | VLEN=256
 Dataset: ../../datasets/issue83_pack_split.dat
============================================================

 How to read this output
   GOPs   [billions of operations per second = 2*M*N*K / time; a multiply-add counts as 2; higher is better]
   Timed  [pack timer + compute timer per rep -- inner timers; gaps between them are excluded; they cost 11-17%: DEBUG build, do not compare to the reference]
   Spread [each rep's own GOPs: the slowest, 5th percentile, 95th percentile and fastest rep, the width of the middle 50%,
           the standard deviation, and the fastest-to-median ratio; a wide spread means unstable timing]
   Full definitions of every log column: docs/metrics.md

============================================================
 Benchmark Config: Arch=jbpi2 | Datatype=fp32 | Method=AXPY_FP32_Pack | VLEN=256
 Dataset: ../../datasets/issue83_pack_split.dat
============================================================

 How to read this output
   GOPs   [billions of operations per second = 2*M*N*K / time; a multiply-add counts as 2; higher is better]
   Timed  [pack timer + compute timer per rep -- inner timers; gaps between them are excluded; they cost 11-17%: DEBUG build, do not compare to the reference]
   Spread [each rep's own GOPs: the slowest, 5th percentile, 95th percentile and fastest rep, the width of the middle 50%,
           the standard deviation, and the fastest-to-median ratio; a wide spread means unstable timing]
   Full definitions of every log column: docs/metrics.md

============================================================
 Benchmark Config: Arch=jbpi2 | Datatype=fp32 | Method=AXPY_FP32_Pack | VLEN=256
 Dataset: ../../datasets/issue83_pack_split.dat

 How to read this output
   GOPs   [billions of operations per second = 2*M*N*K / time; a multiply-add counts as 2; higher is better]
   Timed  [pack timer + compute timer per rep -- inner timers; gaps between them are excluded; they cost 11-17%: DEBUG build, do not compare to the reference]
   Spread [each rep's own GOPs: the slowest, 5th percentile, 95th percentile and fastest rep, the width of the middle 50%,
           the standard deviation, and the fastest-to-median ratio; a wide spread means unstable timing]
   Full definitions of every log column: docs/metrics.md

 How to read this output
   GOPs   [billions of operations per second = 2*M*N*K / time; a multiply-add counts as 2; higher is better]
   Timed  [pack timer + compute timer per rep -- inner timers; gaps between them are excluded; they cost 11-17%: DEBUG build, do not compare to the reference]
   Spread [each rep's own GOPs: the slowest, 5th percentile, 95th percentile and fastest rep, the width of the middle 50%,
           the standard deviation, and the fastest-to-median ratio; a wide spread means unstable timing]
   Full definitions of every log column: docs/metrics.md

============================================================
 Benchmark Config: Arch=jbpi2 | Datatype=fp32 | Method=AXPY_FP32_Pack | VLEN=256
 Dataset: ../../datasets/issue83_pack_split.dat
============================================================

 How to read this output
   GOPs   [billions of operations per second = 2*M*N*K / time; a multiply-add counts as 2; higher is better]
   Timed  [pack timer + compute timer per rep -- inner timers; gaps between them are excluded; they cost 11-17%: DEBUG build, do not compare to the reference]
   Spread [each rep's own GOPs: the slowest, 5th percentile, 95th percentile and fastest rep, the width of the middle 50%,
           the standard deviation, and the fastest-to-median ratio; a wide spread means unstable timing]
   Full definitions of every log column: docs/metrics.md

============================================================

 Dataset: ../../datasets/issue83_pack_split.dat
============================================================

 How to read this output
   GOPs   [billions of operations per second = 2*M*N*K / time; a multiply-add counts as 2; higher is better]
   Timed  [pack timer + compute timer per rep -- inner timers; gaps between them are excluded; they cost 11-17%: DEBUG build, do not compare to the reference]
   Spread [each rep's own GOPs: the slowest, 5th percentile, 95th percentile and fastest rep, the width of the middle 50%,
           the standard deviation, and the fastest-to-median ratio; a wide spread means unstable timing]
   Full definitions of every log column: docs/metrics.md

============================================================
 Benchmark Config: Arch=jbpi2 | Datatype=fp32 | Method=AXPY_FP32_Pack | VLEN=256
 Dataset: ../../datasets/issue83_pack_split.dat
============================================================

 How to read this output
   GOPs   [billions of operations per second = 2*M*N*K / time; a multiply-add counts as 2; higher is better]
   Timed  [pack timer + compute timer per rep -- inner timers; gaps between them are excluded; they cost 11-17%: DEBUG build, do not compare to the reference]
   Spread [each rep's own GOPs: the slowest, 5th percentile, 95th percentile and fastest rep, the width of the middle 50%,
           the standard deviation, and the fastest-to-median ratio; a wide spread means unstable timing]
   Full definitions of every log column: docs/metrics.md

 How to read this output
   GOPs   [billions of operations per second = 2*M*N*K / time; a multiply-add counts as 2; higher is better]
   Timed  [pack timer + compute timer per rep -- inner timers; gaps between them are excluded; they cost 11-17%: DEBUG build, do not compare to the reference]
   Spread [each rep's own GOPs: the slowest, 5th percentile, 95th percentile and fastest rep, the width of the middle 50%,
           the standard deviation, and the fastest-to-median ratio; a wide spread means unstable timing]
   Full definitions of every log column: docs/metrics.md

  |                     back-end stall = share of cycles waiting on the pipeline back end; FLOPs/cycle = operations per cycle]
  |    vector pack       : IPC 0.33 | L1D miss 4.6% read / 2.6% write | back-end stall 76.5%
  |    scalar pack       : IPC 0.33 | L1D miss 4.6% read / 2.6% write | back-end stall 76.5%
  |    compute           : IPC 0.62 | L1D miss 1.3% read / 1.3% write | back-end stall 48.9% | FLOPs/cycle 5.46
  +- ⏭️ SKIP [correctness not checked: skip_ref_run in config.json] [cache blocking: mc=768 rows of A per block, nc=1000 columns of B per block, kc=128 depth of each block]

============================================================

 Dataset: ../../datasets/issue83_pack_split.dat

 How to read this output
   GOPs   [billions of operations per second = 2*M*N*K / time; a multiply-add counts as 2; higher is better]
   Timed  [pack timer + compute timer per rep -- inner timers; gaps between them are excluded; they cost 11-17%: DEBUG build, do not compare to the reference]
   Spread [each rep's own GOPs: the slowest, 5th percentile, 95th percentile and fastest rep, the width of the middle 50%,
           the standard deviation, and the fastest-to-median ratio; a wide spread means unstable timing]
   Full definitions of every log column: docs/metrics.md

 Dataset: ../../datasets/issue83_pack_split.dat

 How to read this output
   GOPs   [billions of operations per second = 2*M*N*K / time; a multiply-add counts as 2; higher is better]
   Timed  [pack timer + compute timer per rep -- inner timers; gaps between them are excluded; they cost 11-17%: DEBUG build, do not compare to the reference]
   Spread [each rep's own GOPs: the slowest, 5th percentile, 95th percentile and fastest rep, the width of the middle 50%,
           the standard deviation, and the fastest-to-median ratio; a wide spread means unstable timing]
   Full definitions of every log column: docs/metrics.md

============================================================

 Dataset: ../../datasets/issue83_pack_split.dat
============================================================

 How to read this output
   GOPs   [billions of operations per second = 2*M*N*K / time; a multiply-add counts as 2; higher is better]
   Timed  [pack timer + compute timer per rep -- inner timers; gaps between them are excluded; they cost 11-17%: DEBUG build, do not compare to the reference]
   Spread [each rep's own GOPs: the slowest, 5th percentile, 95th percentile and fastest rep, the width of the middle 50%,
           the standard deviation, and the fastest-to-median ratio; a wide spread means unstable timing]
   Full definitions of every log column: docs/metrics.md

============================================================

 Dataset: ../../datasets/issue83_pack_split.dat

 How to read this output
   GOPs   [billions of operations per second = 2*M*N*K / time; a multiply-add counts as 2; higher is better]
   Timed  [pack timer + compute timer per rep -- inner timers; gaps between them are excluded; they cost 11-17%: DEBUG build, do not compare to the reference]
   Spread [each rep's own GOPs: the slowest, 5th percentile, 95th percentile and fastest rep, the width of the middle 50%,
           the standard deviation, and the fastest-to-median ratio; a wide spread means unstable timing]
   Full definitions of every log column: docs/metrics.md

============================================================
 Benchmark Config: Arch=jbpi2 | Datatype=fp32 | Method=AXPY_FP32_Pack | VLEN=256
 Dataset: ../../datasets/issue83_pack_split.dat
============================================================

 How to read this output
   GOPs   [billions of operations per second = 2*M*N*K / time; a multiply-add counts as 2; higher is better]
   Timed  [pack timer + compute timer per rep -- inner timers; gaps between them are excluded; they cost 11-17%: DEBUG build, do not compare to the reference]
   Spread [each rep's own GOPs: the slowest, 5th percentile, 95th percentile and fastest rep, the width of the middle 50%,
           the standard deviation, and the fastest-to-median ratio; a wide spread means unstable timing]
   Full definitions of every log column: docs/metrics.md

============================================================

 Dataset: ../../datasets/issue83_pack_split.dat

 How to read this output
   GOPs   [billions of operations per second = 2*M*N*K / time; a multiply-add counts as 2; higher is better]
   Timed  [pack timer + compute timer per rep -- inner timers; gaps between them are excluded; they cost 11-17%: DEBUG build, do not compare to the reference]
   Spread [each rep's own GOPs: the slowest, 5th percentile, 95th percentile and fastest rep, the width of the middle 50%,
           the standard deviation, and the fastest-to-median ratio; a wide spread means unstable timing]
   Full definitions of every log column: docs/metrics.md
